问题与改进

每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38089375339 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 2

重要指引错误上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · 授权 Skill 的 seed 示例要求从共享 seed-data 模块导入，实际运行时报 ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/db · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

授权 Skill 把“共享 seed-data 模块 + 从 seed 以 .js 说明符跨文件导入”写成标准做法（E29/E30），而 @nocobase/db 的任务加载器只用原生动态 import()、只为任务文件自身改写扩展名（E24/E25），seed 内部的相对导入因此按字面解析。修复阶段真实产生与文档示例完全对应的 ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND（E31），最终实现只能改为自包含并内联常量（E75/E76），且与 nocobase-db 的 Self-contained 规则（E28）重新统一。契约、实际行为、应用侧、环境与现有能力均指向同一结论，故 guidance-gap 成立。

  • 职责与公开约定：code-and-seeds.md 第 34-42 行要求把声明放 server/sales-resources.ts、权限集放 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，并给出以 ../../server/sales-resources.js 导入的示例；第 60-97 行的 seed 示例从 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js 导入，并写明 seed 位于 database/main/seeds/ 而导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts（E29/E30）。同一仓库的 nocobase-db Skill 第 129-137 行又规定迁移/seed 必须 Self-contained、不得导入会演变的共享常量（E28）。两处具体承诺互相冲突，构成明确契约。 E29 E30 E28
  • 实际行为证据：task-loader.js 的 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只匹配以 ./ 或 ../ 开头的路径，对 pathToFileURL 产生的 file:// 绝对 URL 实际是 no-op，importTaskDefinition 最终执行原生 import()（E24 第 1-14 行、E25 第 57-61 行）；seed/loader.js 的 loadSeedFile 与 app-server 的 seeder 都经 importTaskDefinition 加载（E26 第 52-67 行、E27 第 24 与 49-51 行）。所以 seed 内部的 ../../seed-data/x.js 不被改写，由 Node 原生 TS 加载器按字面路径解析。修复阶段实测应用启动失败：Database seeds failed for connection "main"，Caused by ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: .../database/seed-data/library-accounts.js imported from .../database/main/seeds/202609210001_library_accounts.ts（E31 第 1269-1271 行），正是文档示例的相对位置。 E24 E25 E26 E27 E31
  • 应用接入与业务边界：本搭建按示例在 database/main/seeds/ 中建立 seed 并跨文件导入，应用启动即报错（E31）。最终实现放弃 database/seed-data 目录，把资源常量、权限集、规则常量内联进每个 seed，并注释说明 seed 是快照、只能在磁盘上字面存在（E75 第 14-19、53-69 行；E76 第 12-21 行）；当前仓库已无 database/seed-data 目录。应用侧没有其它前置条件可补救该导入，只能改自包含写法。 E31 E75 E76
  • 环境因素：失败机制完全在框架源码内：importTaskDefinition 用原生 import()，且扩展名改写 helper 对 file:// 绝对 URL 不生效（E24/E25），报错路径与文档约定的相对位置逐一对应（E31）。不涉及外部服务、凭据或依赖安装；Node 原生 TypeScript 加载器是该产品既定运行环境，不能作为忽略文档冲突的理由。未发现任何环境材料更能解释现象。 E24 E25 E31
  • 工厂因素：冲突示例来自随应用分发的 Skill 文本本身（E29/E30），不是工厂注入提示、QA 脚本或模板覆盖；未发现相反指令。就本候选而言，直接来源是文档承诺与加载器实现不一致，工厂环节未被证明是原因。 E29 E30
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：已存在且可用的能力是自包含 seed：nocobase-db 本已规定 Self-contained（E28），最终实现的内联 seed 正常表达相同数据并注释了原因（E75/E76）。跨文件导入并非满足权限初始化需求的必要条件，因此文档应移除该示例并与之统一，而不是把它当作能力缺口。 E28 E75 E76

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

code-and-seeds.md（第 36/69/97 行）把“共享 seed-data 模块 + .js 说明符导入”作为标准做法；而 @nocobase/db 的 importTaskDefinition 只用原生动态 import() 加载任务，且仅改写任务文件自身的扩展名，Node 原生 TS 加载器只按字面路径解析，故 seed 内部的 ../../seed-data/x.js 找不到实际存在的 .ts。该冲突还直接抵触 nocobase-db Skill 第 131 行的 Self-contained 规则。最终实现不得不把权限集与资源常量内联进各 seed 才通过。

评审时 · 文档 / API 声称

可以把声明放在 server/sales-resources.ts、权限集放在 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，并从 seed 以 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js 导入。

评审时 · 实际观察

seed 运行时不解析 .js→.ts：应用启动时 Database seeds failed，Caused by ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: .../database/seed-data/library-accounts.js imported from .../database/main/seeds/202609210001_library_accounts.ts。

实际影响
按官方 Skill 实施会直接导致应用初始化失败、seed 不执行，并消耗一次完整的修复轮次；正确做法只能靠 Agent 从报错中反推。
建议改法
二选一并保持一致：让 seed 加载器为任务内部相对导入提供 .ts 解析（并说明），或修正 code-and-seeds.md，移除跨文件导入示例、明确要求 seed 自包含并与 nocobase-db 的规则统一。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
按 code-and-seeds.md 在 database/main/seeds/*.ts 中 import { ... } from '../../seed-data/xxx.js'（磁盘上只有 .ts）。
应有能力 / 行为
seed 正常加载并写入初始权限配置。
实际观察
ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND，Database seeds failed for connection "main"，应用无法启动/seed 不执行。
应用绕行与代价
把权限集/资源常量内联进每个 seed，保持自包含（最终实现采用）。
改进后的验收标准
修正 Skill（或加载器）后，文档中的示例可原样通过 pnpm nocobase db apply，且授权 Skill 与 nocobase-db 的自包含规则不再冲突。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 6 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:34–42E29

建议把声明放 server/sales-resources.ts、把权限集值放 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，并给出以 ../../server/sales-resources.js 说明符导入的示例。

## Share declarations, not runtime instances

Keep a portable feature module such as `server/sales-resources.ts` with `defineCompositeResource` declarations. Put permission-set values in `database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts`, importing the same resource references. The corresponding code for a customer-owned feature can live under its own `sales/` directory. Use that feature's names consistently.

```ts
import { definePermissionSet } from '@nocobase/authorization/permission-sets';
import { quotes } from '../../server/sales-resources.js';

export const engineer = definePermissionSet('sales-engineer')
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:60–97E30

seed 示例从 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js 导入权限集，并明确写“seed 位于 database/main/seeds/... 且导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts”。

## A normal App seed has a database context

`defineSeed` from `@nocobase/db` receives `{ query, connection }`. `connection` is a restricted `SeedConnection`, not a complete runtime `DatabaseConnection`; the seed has no application container. Do not cast it to construct an App authorization service or assume `authorizationToken` can be resolved there.

For initial installation, a seed may write the documented persistence rows below after the owning plugins' migrations. Keep this adapter confined to installation data, use fluent values as its input, and test against the installed schema. Runtime routes must use the services instead. Keep one seed-data module per table.

```ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';
import { encodeAuthorizationTitle } from '@nocobase/authorization/core';
import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609190001_sales_jobs',
  transaction: true,
  async run({ query }) {
    const existing = await query
      .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .select('id')
      .where('key', '=', engineer.key)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    if (existing) return; // Preserve an administrator's existing definition.
    const now = new Date();
    await query
      .insertInto('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .values({
        id: engineer.key,
        key: engineer.key,
        title: encodeAuthorizationTitle(engineer.title),
        grants: JSON.stringify(engineer.grants),
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      })
      .execute();
  },
});
```

This seed lives in `database/main/seeds/202609190001_sales_jobs.ts` and imports `database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts`. Adapt the path to the App's configured seed directory; the seed name matches the filename. Ensure schema migrations for authorization and business collections have run, and explicitly order prerequisite installation data. Use the App's existing migration/seed commands. Do not run this code in a migration: migrations must describe fixed schema history and cannot import live business declarations.
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–14E24

DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 同时接受 .js/.mjs/.cjs/.ts；__rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只用于改写任务文件自身的扩展名。

var __rewriteRelativeImportExtension = (this && this.__rewriteRelativeImportExtension) || function (path, preserveJsx) {
    if (typeof path === "string" && /^\.\.?\//.test(path)) {
        return path.replace(/\.(tsx)$|((?:\.d)?)((?:\.[^./]+?)?)\.([cm]?)ts$/i, function (m, tsx, d, ext, cm) {
            return tsx ? preserveJsx ? ".jsx" : ".js" : d && (!ext || !cm) ? m : (d + ext + "." + cm.toLowerCase() + "js");
        });
    }
    return path;
};
import { readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { basename, extname } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
export const DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS = ['.js', '.mjs', '.cjs', '.ts'];
export const DEFAULT_TASK_PACKAGE_NAME = 'app';
/** A directory and a source array are two ways to say the same thing. */
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:50–63E25

importTaskDefinition 用 pathToFileURL + 原生动态 import() 加载任务定义，mtime 查询串避免缓存；不为任务内部的相对导入提供 .js→.ts 解析。

    }
    return extensions.has(extname(fileName));
}
export function taskNameFromFileName(fileName) {
    return basename(fileName, extname(fileName));
}
/** The mtime query keeps a re-read after an edit from serving a cached module. */
export async function importTaskDefinition(filePath, mtimeMs) {
    const url = pathToFileURL(filePath);
    url.searchParams.set('mtime', String(Math.trunc(mtimeMs)));
    const module = await import(__rewriteRelativeImportExtension(url.href));
    return module.default;
}

 * The confidentiality invariant: for `阅读者乙`, only non-confidential records
 * are ever reached by the library's view/edit/delete actions. A restriction

 *

 * runtime declarations on purpose: seeds are run both from source (under Node's
 * native TypeScript loader, which resolves only the specifiers that literally

### [重要][指引错误] 授权 Skill 的 seed 示例要求从共享 seed-data 模块导入，实际运行时报 ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.17）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

授权 Skill 把“共享 seed-data 模块 + 从 seed 以 .js 说明符跨文件导入”写成标准做法（E29/E30），而 @nocobase/db 的任务加载器只用原生动态 import()、只为任务文件自身改写扩展名（E24/E25），seed 内部的相对导入因此按字面解析。修复阶段真实产生与文档示例完全对应的 ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND（E31），最终实现只能改为自包含并内联常量（E75/E76），且与 nocobase-db 的 Self-contained 规则（E28）重新统一。契约、实际行为、应用侧、环境与现有能力均指向同一结论，故 guidance-gap 成立。

- 职责与公开约定：code-and-seeds.md 第 34-42 行要求把声明放 server/sales-resources.ts、权限集放 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，并给出以 ../../server/sales-resources.js 导入的示例；第 60-97 行的 seed 示例从 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js 导入，并写明 seed 位于 database/main/seeds/ 而导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts（E29/E30）。同一仓库的 nocobase-db Skill 第 129-137 行又规定迁移/seed 必须 Self-contained、不得导入会演变的共享常量（E28）。两处具体承诺互相冲突，构成明确契约。；证据：E29（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:34–42）、E30（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:60–97）、E28（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:129–137）
- 实际行为证据：task-loader.js 的 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只匹配以 ./ 或 ../ 开头的路径，对 pathToFileURL 产生的 file:// 绝对 URL 实际是 no-op，importTaskDefinition 最终执行原生 import()（E24 第 1-14 行、E25 第 57-61 行）；seed/loader.js 的 loadSeedFile 与 app-server 的 seeder 都经 importTaskDefinition 加载（E26 第 52-67 行、E27 第 24 与 49-51 行）。所以 seed 内部的 ../../seed-data/x.js 不被改写，由 Node 原生 TS 加载器按字面路径解析。修复阶段实测应用启动失败：Database seeds failed for connection "main"，Caused by ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: .../database/seed-data/library-accounts.js imported from .../database/main/seeds/202609210001_library_accounts.ts（E31 第 1269-1271 行），正是文档示例的相对位置。；证据：E24（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–14）、E25（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:50–63）、E26（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/seed/loader.js:6–40）、E27（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/database/seeder.js:6–30）、E31（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-1.jsonl/part-0042.txt:1269–1271）
- 应用接入与业务边界：本搭建按示例在 database/main/seeds/ 中建立 seed 并跨文件导入，应用启动即报错（E31）。最终实现放弃 database/seed-data 目录，把资源常量、权限集、规则常量内联进每个 seed，并注释说明 seed 是快照、只能在磁盘上字面存在（E75 第 14-19、53-69 行；E76 第 12-21 行）；当前仓库已无 database/seed-data 目录。应用侧没有其它前置条件可补救该导入，只能改自包含写法。；证据：E31（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-1.jsonl/part-0042.txt:1269–1271）、E75（app/database/main/seeds/202609210003_library_permission_sets.ts:21–70）、E76（app/database/main/seeds/202609210004_library_restrictions.ts:1–30）
- 环境因素：失败机制完全在框架源码内：importTaskDefinition 用原生 import()，且扩展名改写 helper 对 file:// 绝对 URL 不生效（E24/E25），报错路径与文档约定的相对位置逐一对应（E31）。不涉及外部服务、凭据或依赖安装；Node 原生 TypeScript 加载器是该产品既定运行环境，不能作为忽略文档冲突的理由。未发现任何环境材料更能解释现象。；证据：E24（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–14）、E25（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:50–63）、E31（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-1.jsonl/part-0042.txt:1269–1271）
- 工厂因素：冲突示例来自随应用分发的 Skill 文本本身（E29/E30），不是工厂注入提示、QA 脚本或模板覆盖；未发现相反指令。就本候选而言，直接来源是文档承诺与加载器实现不一致，工厂环节未被证明是原因。；证据：E29（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:34–42）、E30（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:60–97）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：已存在且可用的能力是自包含 seed：nocobase-db 本已规定 Self-contained（E28），最终实现的内联 seed 正常表达相同数据并注释了原因（E75/E76）。跨文件导入并非满足权限初始化需求的必要条件，因此文档应移除该示例并与之统一，而不是把它当作能力缺口。；证据：E28（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:129–137）、E75（app/database/main/seeds/202609210003_library_permission_sets.ts:21–70）、E76（app/database/main/seeds/202609210004_library_restrictions.ts:1–30）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

code-and-seeds.md（第 36/69/97 行）把“共享 seed-data 模块 + .js 说明符导入”作为标准做法；而 @nocobase/db 的 importTaskDefinition 只用原生动态 import() 加载任务，且仅改写任务文件自身的扩展名，Node 原生 TS 加载器只按字面路径解析，故 seed 内部的 ../../seed-data/x.js 找不到实际存在的 .ts。该冲突还直接抵触 nocobase-db Skill 第 131 行的 Self-contained 规则。最终实现不得不把权限集与资源常量内联进各 seed 才通过。

**文档 / API 声称**：可以把声明放在 server/sales-resources.ts、权限集放在 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，并从 seed 以 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js 导入。

**实际观察**：seed 运行时不解析 .js→.ts：应用启动时 Database seeds failed，Caused by ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: .../database/seed-data/library-accounts.js imported from .../database/main/seeds/202609210001_library_accounts.ts。

**影响**：按官方 Skill 实施会直接导致应用初始化失败、seed 不执行，并消耗一次完整的修复轮次；正确做法只能靠 Agent 从报错中反推。

**建议改动**：二选一并保持一致：让 seed 加载器为任务内部相对导入提供 .ts 解析（并说明），或修正 code-and-seeds.md，移除跨文件导入示例、明确要求 seed 自包含并与 nocobase-db 的规则统一。

**验收标准**：修正 Skill（或加载器）后，文档中的示例可原样通过 pnpm nocobase db apply，且授权 Skill 与 nocobase-db 的自包含规则不再冲突。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:34–42` — 建议把声明放 server/sales-resources.ts、把权限集值放 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，并给出以 ../../server/sales-resources.js 说明符导入的示例。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:60–97` — seed 示例从 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js 导入权限集，并明确写“seed 位于 database/main/seeds/... 且导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts”。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–14` — DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 同时接受 .js/.mjs/.cjs/.ts；__rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只用于改写任务文件自身的扩展名。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:50–63` — importTaskDefinition 用 pathToFileURL + 原生动态 import() 加载任务定义，mtime 查询串避免缓存；不为任务内部的相对导入提供 .js→.ts 解析。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-1.jsonl/part-0042.txt:1269–1271` — 修复阶段运行逻辑测试时应用启动失败：Database seeds failed，Caused by ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: .../database/seed-data/library-accounts.js imported from .../seeds/202609210001_library_accounts.ts。
- `app/database/main/seeds/202609210004_library_restrictions.ts:1–30` — 保密限制种子为读者账号声明 view/edit/delete 的 recordAccess 限制，注释说明其与 grants/共享/默认访问求交，以及 seed 必须自包含的原因。

F2 · 规则 Skill 未说明 selection.records 只接受字符串 id

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

本条定向复核未通过校验：Supported feedback needs diagnosis and every counter-evidence check

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    parseRecordSelection 要求 records 选择的每个 id 都是非空字符串，否则抛 Invalid record selection。共享/限制 Skill 的示例都用字符串 id，但没有说明这是硬约束；使用整数自增主键的集合将无法按单条记录共享，本搭建最初用 increments('id')，后改为 collection.string('id') 才能共享草稿 D。

    实际影响
    采用整数主键的 Agent 会在共享单条记录时收到与主键类型相关的隐式失败，需要额外排查或改迁移。
    建议改法
    在 sharing/restriction Skill 中显式说明记录选择使用字符串 id（或让选择接受集合主键类型），并在主授权 Skill 的资源声明处提示。

    本条未提供结构化触发条件、绕行与回归标准，请按原文和证据复核，不自动补造。

    评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
    packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/selection.js:1–25E6

    parseRecordSelection：records 选择要求 ids 每个都是非空字符串，否则抛 Invalid record selection；支持 all / records / recordAccess。

    export const selection = {
    all: () => ({ type: 'all' }),
    records: (ids) => ({ type: 'records', ids: [...ids] }),
    recordAccess: (key, params) => params === undefined
        ? { type: 'recordAccess', key }
        : { type: 'recordAccess', key, params: structuredClone(params) },
};
/** Validates an untrusted value, returning it as a detached selection. */
export function parseRecordSelection(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)) {
        const type = Reflect.get(value, 'type');
        if (type === 'all')
            return selection.all();
        const ids = Reflect.get(value, 'ids');
        if (type === 'records' &&
            Array.isArray(ids) &&
            ids.every((id) => typeof id === 'string' && id !== ''))
            return selection.records(ids);
        const key = Reflect.get(value, 'key');
        if (type === 'recordAccess' && typeof key === 'string' && key)
            return selection.recordAccess(key, Reflect.get(value, 'params'));
    }
    throw new TypeError('Invalid record selection');
}
//# sourceMappingURL=selection.js.map
    app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:6–24E13

    共享为“已持有动作的指定主体增加选定记录”，不能选全部；grant、默认访问与共享先合并，限制规则再收窄；流程要求先授予动作、核对每个数据范围。

    # Develop sharing rules

A sharing rule adds selected records, or a record access selection, for the subjects it lists, provided they already hold the action. Sharing does not grant the operation, fields, page access or related records, and it cannot select all records. Grants, default access and sharing combine first; restriction rules then narrow the result.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For sharing between departments, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. A rule whose recipient is a department shares specific records with it; a viewer-relative department scope such as 本部门及下属部门 is computed for the viewer and cannot select another department's records. The guide's optional sharing section covers this plugin, and its core section says what remains with permission sets alone.

## Development workflow

1. Identify the real collaboration exception: who receives which records, for which actions, and why. Use explicit records for one handover; use a record access selection for a maintained region or team rule.
2. Grant the recipients the business action through a permission set first. Register inherited subjects and their active membership resolver through the main authorization Skill.
3. Inspect every data scope the operation requires. Quote submission needs the selected quote and its actual parent project. Add each action and data scope deliberately; view, edit and submit are different permissions.
4. Save a complete rule through the settings page or the service. Keep record ids tied to their data scope's collection and preserve the other entries on update.
5. Verify the handover works, an unshared parent still blocks the workflow, restrictions still exclude confidential records, and sharing alone cannot activate a missing operation.
6. Revoke the recipient or the team's permission set and verify the next request loses only that source. Independent direct-user sharing and direct assignments must survive.

For example, a quote handover shares quote edit and submit plus the parent project's submit scope with a delegated engineer. Use the real record ids, never sample ones.
    app/database/main/migrations/202609200001_create_library_documents.ts:14–40E77

    迁移使用 collection.string('id') 作为主键，注释明确“共享规则按字符串 id 选记录，整数键无法被共享”。

        await builder.createCollection('libraryDocuments', (collection) => {
      // A text primary key, as every collection in this framework uses: a
      // sharing rule selects records by their string id, so an integer key
      // could not be shared.
      collection.string('id', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('title', { length: 255, nullable: false });
      collection.text('content', { nullable: true });
      // The owner is compared in record-access filters, so it is a plain column
      // and not a relation; `ownerName` is denormalized for display so reading a
      // document never needs to join the user table.
      collection.string('ownerId', { length: 255, nullable: false });
      collection.string('ownerName', { length: 255, nullable: true });
      collection.boolean('published', { nullable: false, defaultValue: false });
      collection.boolean('confidential', {
        nullable: false,
        defaultValue: false,
      });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('updatedAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.primary('id', { name: 'pk_library_documents' });
      collection.index('ownerId', { name: 'idx_library_documents_owner' });
      collection.index(['published', 'confidential'], {
        name: 'idx_library_documents_visibility',
      });
    });
  },
    上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
    ### [轻微][易用性改进] 规则 Skill 未说明 selection.records 只接受字符串 id

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/authorization`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.11）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

本条定向复核未通过校验：Supported feedback needs diagnosis and every counter-evidence check


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

parseRecordSelection 要求 records 选择的每个 id 都是非空字符串，否则抛 Invalid record selection。共享/限制 Skill 的示例都用字符串 id，但没有说明这是硬约束；使用整数自增主键的集合将无法按单条记录共享，本搭建最初用 increments('id')，后改为 collection.string('id') 才能共享草稿 D。

**影响**：采用整数主键的 Agent 会在共享单条记录时收到与主键类型相关的隐式失败，需要额外排查或改迁移。

**建议改动**：在 sharing/restriction Skill 中显式说明记录选择使用字符串 id（或让选择接受集合主键类型），并在主授权 Skill 的资源声明处提示。

    实现者过程与运行背景 · 4 个过程问题 / 3 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

    本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

    本轮过程记录

    修复失败验收：补齐资料库(资料员甲/阅读者乙)、权限集、保密限制、共享/撤销、停用账号失效等能力，并使其可通过构建与启动期自检。

    问题 01 · 应用启动与数据初始化

    应用自带 seed 的共享模块在源码运行时无法解析

    本任务已处理

    app-server 测试启动应用时 Database seeds failed：Cannot find module '<root>/database/seed-data/library-accounts.js' imported from database/main/seeds/202609210001_library_accounts.ts；仅 1 个用户、无乙、无资料数据的验收失败由此产生。

    原因（实现者自述）
    应用 seed 由运行时在打包器之外动态加载，走 Node 原生 TS 类型擦除；它不会把 `.js` 说明符改写为 `.ts`。因此 `database/main/seeds/*` 相对导入 `database/seed-data/*.js` 只在有改写器（CLI 开发加载器 / 编译产物）时才成立，源码运行直接报 ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND。
    处理方式
    把四个 seed 改为自包含（数据内联，权限 grants 与限制规则的资源标识以字面量写出），删除 database/seed-data/，routes.ts 中引用该目录的注释同步更新；源码运行与编译产物两处均可执行。
    影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
    问题 02 · 应用启动与数据初始化

    LibraryProvider.boot 在未注册 authorization 插件的最小运行时崩溃

    本任务已处理

    app-server 测试 'registers application-owned providers and routes from the runtime' 启动失败：Service "@nocobase/app/authorization" is not registered. at LibraryProvider.boot server/providers/library.ts:45。

    原因（实现者自述）
    该测试以 plugins: [] 组装运行时，授权插件缺席；LibraryProvider.boot() 无条件 container.resolve(authorizationToken)。
    处理方式
    boot() 先 container.has(authorizationToken) 判断，缺席即早退（资料库能力在该运行时为惰性），与 register() 对 databaseManagerToken 的判断一致。
    影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
    问题 03 · 共享规则可见性

    自增整数主键无法被共享规则选中

    本任务已处理

    管理员对草稿 D 建共享规则后，乙仍不可见；对整型 id 的过滤在 Repository 过滤器校验处返回 400 INVALID_FILTER（Filter value is invalid for Field "id" of type "integer"）。

    原因（实现者自述）
    共享规则以字符串 id 记录所选记录（selection.records 要求每个 id 为 string），而 libraryDocuments.id 为 integer 自增，两套标识无法互操作。
    处理方式
    迁移改为 collection.string('id', { length: 64 }) + collection.primary；服务端用 crypto.randomUUID() 生成 id，seed 用固定 UUID 字符串；同时把 'id' 加入 CREATE_FIELDS，使创建策略允许写入生成的 id（否则 403 FIELD_WRITE_FORBIDDEN）。
    影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
    问题 04 · 保密资料不可见

    保密资料仅靠共享规则不足以保证乙不可见

    本任务已处理

    把保密资料 C 共享给乙后，需要确认乙仍 404，而甲与管理员不受影响。

    原因（实现者自述）
    共享规则是授权合并（并集）的一部分，无法单独表达'永不放开'；需要一条只作用于乙、无法被授予放宽的限制规则来收窄。
    处理方式
    新增 composite 限制规则 library.nonConfidential（view/edit/delete，scopeKey=libraryDocuments，selection.recordAccess('library.nonConfidential')），仅分配给乙。限制与共享求交，故共享无法泄露保密资料；甲与管理员非该规则主体，访问不变。
    影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

    实现者补充建议

    来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

    建议 01 · 测试与自检

    应用 seed 的源码运行约束应在模板层强制

    可自动化

    应用 seed/migration 在打包器之外按 Node 原生 TS 运行，任何指向应用自身其它 TS 模块的 `.js` 相对导入都会让 app-server 启动期 seed 安装失败，且只在特定测试路径暴露。

    建议改动

    在模板测试或构建检查中加入一条规则：database/<connection>/{seeds,migrations} 下的文件不得相对导入应用自身的 .ts 源模块（只允许包导入或本地文件），并在 AGENTS.md 说明 seed 必须自包含。

    来源：Agent · retro.json

    建议 02 · 测试与自检

    API 文档声明校验未纳入既有测试

    可自动化

    AGENTS.md 要求测试断言 findUndeclaredApiRoutes()/findApiDocumentSchemaProblems() 为空，但模板与已有测试都没有调用它们，新增路由不会因此被拦住。

    建议改动

    在默认 app-server 测试模板中加入这两条断言，使任何缺少 describeRoute/apiValidator 的路由在 CI 即失败。

    来源：Agent · retro.json

    建议 03 · 测试与自检

    登录测试助手的会话 fetch 未带可信 Origin，cookie 写请求被 CSRF 拦截

    需人工判断

    signIn() 返回的 session.fetch 不带 Origin；认证插件对带 cookie 的写请求要求可信 Origin，于是测试中的 POST/PATCH/DELETE 以 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN 失败，且与'无权限'的 403 同码，容易误判。

    建议改动

    让 signIn 的 fetch 在不安全方法上默认带上与 APP_PUBLIC_ORIGIN 一致的可信 Origin，或在 Skill/文档中明确要求测试对写请求显式设置 Origin。

    来源：Agent · retro.json

    NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

    做得好的地方
    F3 · 限制规则与正范围取交，保密约束不会被共享或默认访问绕过

    NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · @nocobase/authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules/SKILL.md

    ⌄

    authorizer 先求正范围（权限集 recordAccess、默认访问、共享等 expand 约束），再执行 scope: allScopes([...required, ...restrictions])；restriction kind 的 effect 为 restrict。因此即便给读者共享了保密资料 C，限制仍将其排除，而拥有者/管理员不作为规则主体不受影响（B04 通过）。

    提供的帮助
    为“误共享也不能越过保密边界”提供了结构性保证，而非依赖配置正确。
    应保留的能力
    保持该求交语义，并继续在规则 Skill 中强调“选择描述仍允许的记录”。
    E3 E7 E14 E76
    做得好的地方
    F4 · 停用账号同时失效既有会话

    NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · @nocobase/app-plugin-users · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md

    ⌄

    disable() 写入 disabledAt 并调用 revokeSessions()→deleteUserSessions(userId)+disconnectUser；getSession 读取会话后复查 disabledAt 并返回 null，会话创建钩子也拒绝停用账号。users 路由暴露 /users/:userId/disable 与 /revokeSessions（B05 通过）。

    提供的帮助
    管理员停用离职账号后，既有登录立即失去访问，无需额外部署或手工清理。
    应保留的能力
    保持 disabledAt 单一状态源与三处校验的一致性。
    E18 E19 E22 E23
    做得好的地方
    F5 · 仓储策略只会收窄，无法越权放宽

    NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · @nocobase/db

    ⌄

    narrowRepositoryPolicy 明确“没有可放宽的写法”：scope 取 AND、字段与关系取交集、任一侧 false 即 false；enforce 在策略节点为 false 时直接抛 READ_FORBIDDEN/WRITE_FORBIDDEN。业务侧把授权决策编译为 policy 后，仓储调用无法绕过已收窄的范围与字段。

    提供的帮助
    为字段级与服务端强制提供了可靠下限，阅读/编辑分离不会因仓储调用而泄漏。
    应保留的能力
    保持“只减不增”的单调收窄语义。

    E1 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:105–135

    authorizeWithGrants：无资源处理器即 denied，unrestricted 身份走 authorizeUnrestricted，否则交给资源处理器；conditional 缺 conditions 直接 denied；异常统一转 AUTHORIZATION_HANDLER_FAILED。

        async authorizeWithGrants(request, grants, constraints, resolveConditions = true) {
        const handler = resourceTypeHandler(this.resourceTypes, request.resource.type);
        if (!handler) {
            return deny('UNKNOWN_RESOURCE_TYPE', `No resource type "${request.resource.type}" is registered`);
        }
        const stored = request;
        try {
            const unrestricted = (await grants.unrestricted?.({
                principal: request.principal,
                ...(request.subjects === undefined
                    ? {}
                    : { subjects: request.subjects }),
            })) === true;
            const decision = unrestricted
                ? await handler.authorizeUnrestricted(stored)
                : await handler.authorize(stored, { grants, constraints });
            if (decision.effect === 'conditional' &&
                decision.conditions === undefined) {
                return deny('INVALID_CONDITIONAL_DECISION', `Resource type "${handler.type}" returned a conditional decision without conditions`);
            }
            if (!resolveConditions ||
                decision.effect === 'deny' ||
                request.resource.type !== COMPOSITE_RESOURCE_TYPE)
                return decision;
            return await this.composeComposite(stored, decision, grants, constraints);
        }
        catch (error) {
            return deny('AUTHORIZATION_HANDLER_FAILED', error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Authorization handler failed');
        }
    }
    /** Checks each grant target a composite action composes, with its grants only. */

    原文件 SHA-256：2f78a83ee3a627130e13817d627ebffcd44bb72571349c9fb4490d33ac10a708

    E2 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/composite.js:420–458

    composedGrants：把复合资源的 grant 展开为各目标资源动作并绑定 scopeKey 分支约束，复合动作只组合它列出的 grant。

    export function composedGrants(provider, registry, constraints) {
    const expand = (grants, input) => Promise.all(grants
        .flatMap((grant) => registry.expandOrSkip(grant))
        .map(async (grant) => {
        if (grant.origin?.scopeKey === undefined)
            return grant;
        const branch = await constraints.resolve({
            ...input,
            resource: grant.origin.resource,
            action: grant.origin.action,
            scopeKey: grant.origin.scopeKey,
        });
        return { ...grant, origin: { ...grant.origin, constraints: branch } };
    }));
    return {
        resolveAll: async (input) => expand(await provider.resolveAll(input), input),
        resolve: async (input) => {
            const direct = await provider.resolve(input);
            if (input.resource.type === COMPOSITE_RESOURCE_TYPE) {
                // The composite handler denies a grant that no longer expands.
                for (const grant of direct) {
                    const reason = registry.validateGrant(grant);
                    if (reason !== undefined)
                        registry.report(grant, reason);
                }
                return direct;
            }
            if (!registry.size)
                return direct;
            const composites = (await provider.resolveAll(input)).filter((grant) => grant.resource.type === COMPOSITE_RESOURCE_TYPE);
            const composed = (await expand(composites, input)).filter((grant) => grant.resource.type === input.resource.type &&
                (grant.resource.id === '*' ||
                    grant.resource.id === input.resource.id) &&
                grant.action === input.action);
            return [...direct, ...composed];
        },
        ...(provider.unrestricted
            ? { unrestricted: provider.unrestricted.bind(provider) }
            : {}),

    原文件 SHA-256：dd79a9736febf0fb08562360d1dd62581d8c01bd6687265f096e4c2981e0da13

    E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/authorizer.js:205–246

    resolveEffectiveScope 先求正范围（recordAccess/expand 约束），再把 restrict 约束与 required 一起 allScopes 求交，最终 scope: allScopes([...required, ...restrictions])。

        }
    async resolveEffectiveScope(principal, resource, action, configs, constraints, requestedFields) {
        // Each grant keeps its own fields and role filters through scope evaluation.
        const branches = await Promise.all(configs.map(async (config) => {
            const positive = await this.compileScopes(principal, resource, action, config.recordAccess ?? []);
            positive.push(...(await this.compileConstraints([...constraints, ...(config.branchConstraints ?? [])].filter((item) => item.effect === 'expand'), principal, resource, action)));
            const branchRestrictions = await this.compileConstraints((config.branchConstraints ?? []).filter((item) => item.effect === 'restrict'), principal, resource, action);
            return {
                config,
                configured: positive.length > 0,
                scope: allScopes([anyScope(positive), ...branchRestrictions]),
            };
        }));
        const restrictions = await this.compileConstraints(constraints.filter((item) => item.effect === 'restrict'), principal, resource, action);
        const fields = resolveDatabaseFields(configs, action);
        const requested = {
            ...requestedFields,
            ...(action === 'read'
                ? { output: resolveActionFields(action, fields, resource) }
                : { input: resolveActionFields(action, fields, resource) }),
        };
        const required = [
            anyScope(branches.map((branch) => branch.scope)),
        ];
        for (const direction of [
            'input',
            'output',
            'filter',
            'sort',
            'group',
        ]) {
            for (const field of requested[direction] ?? []) {
                if (branches.every((branch) => databaseFieldsAllowed({ [direction]: [field] }, resolveDatabaseFields([branch.config], action))))
                    continue;
                required.push(anyScope(branches
                    .filter((branch) => databaseFieldsAllowed({ [direction]: [field] }, resolveDatabaseFields([branch.config], action)))
                    .map((branch) => branch.scope)));
            }
        }
        return {
            scope: allScopes([...required, ...restrictions]),
            configured: branches.some((branch) => branch.configured),

    E4 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:55–64

    “Implement in this order”：先自包含迁移声明结构；用 defineDatabasePermission / defineCompositeResource 声明动作、字段与每个集合；provider boot 注册集合、复合资源、record access 与 settings；路由先鉴权再校验。

    ## Implement in this order

1. Declare schema and relationships through self-contained migrations. Choose stable composite, page and data scope names. Keep authorization decisions out of migration code.
2. Declare reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission`; compose user-facing business operations as composite resources with `defineCompositeResource` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`. Explicitly name fields, relation capabilities and every collection a workflow touches.
3. In the owning provider's `boot`, opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add`, register composites with `authz.compositeResources.define`, record access with `authz.recordAccess.define` and settings items with `authz.settings.add`. Then add a workspace subsection with `authz.ui.sections.add({ name, title, parent })` and list each composite or settings item in it with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section, group? })` (the permission workspace lists sections and subsections on the left and the selected subsection's resources, under optional `authz.ui.groups`, on the right; an unplaced composite or settings item goes under its default section's Other with a startup warning, and a misplaced one fails a development start). Pages are not registered on the server: the client route tree lists them. Release subject registrations at shutdown.
4. Protect every server route with authentication. For a simple single-scope Repository operation, keep `defineRepositoryApiRoutes` and add `authz.database.authorizeRepository` to map methods to composite actions; it reuses or initializes the request's authorization context. For workflows, persisted-state checks, multiple data scopes or custom responses, install `authz.middleware()`, call `c.get('authz').authorize()` once and bind every returned collection policy with `repository.withPolicy()`. Follow [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md).
5. Declare `authz` on every entry client route (nested pages inherit it; an omitted one defaults to root-only or `'skip'` with a development warning), add `useCan` for action visibility and obtain per-record eligibility from the server when needed. Handle pending and error states without displaying stale access.
6. Complete the accompanying permission configuration from the responsibility matrix: sets, page and action grants, selected data scopes, supported rules and intended assignments. Use seeds for fresh installations and authorized provisioning for existing installations; editing a seed does not update a running App. Resolve optional capabilities through their installed Skills and preserve unrelated administrator edits.
7. Verify denied as well as allowed requests through the production route factory and actual database policies. Then verify the UI with ordinary users and inherited memberships.

    E5 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/plugins/internal/rules.js:1–70

    RuleService：validateRule 用 parseRecordSelection 校验规则选择；constraints 按资源与主体匹配规则并产出 effect=expand/restrict 的约束；kind 决定 bySubject/allowAll。

    import { parseRecordSelection } from '../../core/selection.js';
import { resolveAuthorizationSubjects } from '../../core/subjects.js';
/** Rejects a malformed rule before it reaches a store. */
export function validateRule(rule, kind) {
    if (!rule.key)
        throw new TypeError('A rule needs a key');
    if (!rule.resource.type || !rule.resource.id)
        throw new TypeError(`Rule ${rule.key} needs a resource`);
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const action of rule.actions) {
        if (!action.action)
            throw new TypeError(`Rule ${rule.key} has an unnamed action`);
        const id = JSON.stringify([action.action, action.scopeKey ?? null]);
        if (seen.has(id))
            throw new TypeError(`Rule ${rule.key} repeats ${action.action}`);
        seen.add(id);
        const selection = parseRecordSelection(action.selection);
        if (selection.type === 'all' && !kind.allowAll)
            throw new TypeError(`A ${kind.id} rule cannot select all records`);
    }
}
export class RuleService {
    kind;
    store;
    id;
    constructor(kind, store) {
        this.kind = kind;
        this.store = store;
        this.id = kind.id;
    }
    withTransaction(transaction) {
        return new RuleService(this.kind, this.store.withTransaction(transaction));
    }
    async create(rule) {
        validateRule(rule, this.kind);
        await this.assertResourceFree(rule, undefined);
        return this.store.create(rule);
    }
    async update(key, rule) {
        validateRule(rule, this.kind);
        await this.assertResourceFree(rule, key);
        return this.store.update(key, rule);
    }
    async assertResourceFree(rule, replacing) {
        const conflict = this.kind.onePerResource;
        if (!conflict)
            return;
        const existing = (await this.store.list()).find((entry) => entry.key !== replacing &&
            entry.resource.type === rule.resource.type &&
            entry.resource.id === rule.resource.id);
        if (existing)
            throw conflict(rule, existing);
    }
    delete(key) {
        return this.store.delete(key);
    }
    get(key) {
        return this.store.get(key);
    }
    list() {
        return this.store.list();
    }
    /** Reads the rules once per identity context. */
    for() {
        let rules;
        return {
            id: this.id,
            resolve: async (input) => this.constraints(input, await (rules ??= this.store.list())),
        };
    }

    E6 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/selection.js:1–25

    parseRecordSelection：records 选择要求 ids 每个都是非空字符串，否则抛 Invalid record selection；支持 all / records / recordAccess。

    export const selection = {
    all: () => ({ type: 'all' }),
    records: (ids) => ({ type: 'records', ids: [...ids] }),
    recordAccess: (key, params) => params === undefined
        ? { type: 'recordAccess', key }
        : { type: 'recordAccess', key, params: structuredClone(params) },
};
/** Validates an untrusted value, returning it as a detached selection. */
export function parseRecordSelection(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)) {
        const type = Reflect.get(value, 'type');
        if (type === 'all')
            return selection.all();
        const ids = Reflect.get(value, 'ids');
        if (type === 'records' &&
            Array.isArray(ids) &&
            ids.every((id) => typeof id === 'string' && id !== ''))
            return selection.records(ids);
        const key = Reflect.get(value, 'key');
        if (type === 'recordAccess' && typeof key === 'string' && key)
            return selection.recordAccess(key, Reflect.get(value, 'params'));
    }
    throw new TypeError('Invalid record selection');
}

    E13 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:6–24

    共享为“已持有动作的指定主体增加选定记录”，不能选全部；grant、默认访问与共享先合并，限制规则再收窄；流程要求先授予动作、核对每个数据范围。

    # Develop sharing rules

A sharing rule adds selected records, or a record access selection, for the subjects it lists, provided they already hold the action. Sharing does not grant the operation, fields, page access or related records, and it cannot select all records. Grants, default access and sharing combine first; restriction rules then narrow the result.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For sharing between departments, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. A rule whose recipient is a department shares specific records with it; a viewer-relative department scope such as 本部门及下属部门 is computed for the viewer and cannot select another department's records. The guide's optional sharing section covers this plugin, and its core section says what remains with permission sets alone.

## Development workflow

1. Identify the real collaboration exception: who receives which records, for which actions, and why. Use explicit records for one handover; use a record access selection for a maintained region or team rule.
2. Grant the recipients the business action through a permission set first. Register inherited subjects and their active membership resolver through the main authorization Skill.
3. Inspect every data scope the operation requires. Quote submission needs the selected quote and its actual parent project. Add each action and data scope deliberately; view, edit and submit are different permissions.
4. Save a complete rule through the settings page or the service. Keep record ids tied to their data scope's collection and preserve the other entries on update.
5. Verify the handover works, an unshared parent still blocks the workflow, restrictions still exclude confidential records, and sharing alone cannot activate a missing operation.
6. Revoke the recipient or the team's permission set and verify the next request loses only that source. Independent direct-user sharing and direct assignments must survive.

For example, a quote handover shares quote edit and submit plus the parent project's submit scope with a delegated engineer. Use the real record ids, never sample ones.

    E14 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules/SKILL.md:6–23

    限制规则把动作可达记录与规则选择求交，选择描述“仍允许”的记录；多条限制都生效，且都不能授予动作或放宽范围。

    # Develop restriction rules

A restriction rule intersects the records an action reaches with the rule's selection, for the subjects it lists. The selection describes the records still allowed, not the records to hide. Several matching restrictions all apply; none can grant an action or widen a range.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For a restriction assigned to a department, or to the root department for the whole company, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. Its core works with permission sets alone and says how to keep excluded records out without this plugin; its optional restriction section covers this one.

## Development workflow

1. State the invariant positively, for example "this team may access only non-confidential projects". Define who it applies to and whether it covers one operation or every path to a collection.
2. Use a rule on a composite, with `scopeKey`, for an operation-specific limit. For a limit across every business branch, create a rule on the `database.collection` with its CRUD actions; the settings page offers declared business data scopes, and collection-wide invariants can be provisioned through the service.
3. Register a suitable record access and save the complete rule. Apply the restriction directly to a user as well when it must remain after that person's team membership is removed.
4. Bind policies on all protected reads and writes. For relation targets, declare relation record access explicitly; a standalone collection restriction is not inherited by nested relation writes.
5. Verify the excluded row stays inaccessible after adding broad sharing and another permission set, across each protected operation, and that unrelated actors keep their intended access.

For example, a confidentiality restriction assigned directly to each account holds whichever permission set grants the action. Test with ordinary users: unrestricted identities skip every rule.

    E15 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/narrow.js:1–45

    narrowRepositoryPolicy 注释与实现：只做收窄，scope 取 AND、字段与关系取交集、任一侧 false 即 false，没有可放宽的写法。

    import { RepositoryError } from '../errors.js';
import { normalizeRepositoryPolicy } from './normalize.js';
/**
 * Narrow a bound Policy.
 *
 * Every rule here only ever takes away: scopes are ANDed, field lists
 * intersected, relations intersected, and `false` on either side wins. There
 * is deliberately no spelling that widens — a caller holding a narrowed
 * Repository cannot climb back out of it.
 *
 * A patch is a patch: a member it does not mention is left as it was. A member
 * it does mention is intersected with what was there, so `relations: {}` is a
 * way to say "none of them" rather than "no change".
 */
export function narrowRepositoryPolicy(current, patch) {
    assertPatchKeys(patch);
    return Object.freeze({
        read: narrowNode(current.read, patch.read, ['read'], narrowReadNode),
        create: narrowNode(current.create, patch.create, ['create'], narrowCreateNode),
        update: narrowNode(current.update, patch.update, ['update'], (node, item) => narrowWriteNode(node, item, 'update')),
        delete: narrowNode(current.delete, patch.delete, ['delete'], narrowDeleteNode),
    });
}
function invalid(message, path) {
    throw new RepositoryError('INVALID_POLICY', message, { path });
}
function assertPatchKeys(patch) {
    for (const key of Object.keys(patch)) {
        if (!['read', 'create', 'update', 'delete'].includes(key)) {
            invalid(`Unsupported Policy option: ${key}.`, [key]);
        }
    }
}
/**
 * Normalize one patched node by borrowing the full-policy normalizer.
 *
 * A patch node may omit `scope`, which the normalizer requires, so an
 * unrestricted stand-in fills the gap; intersecting with `true` is what an
 * omitted scope means anyway.
 */
function normalizePatchNode(key, node) {
    const open = { scope: true };
    const full = {
        read: open,
        create: open,

    import { invalid, isPlainRecord } from '../internal/guards.js';
/**
 * Resolve the read node, rejecting the call outright when reading is forbidden.
 * Returns `undefined` when no Policy is bound and `true` when it adds no limits.
 */
export function resolveReadNode(policy, collection) {
    const node = policy?.read;
    if (node === false) {
        invalid('READ_FORBIDDEN', 'Reading from this Repository is forbidden by Policy.', { collection: collection.name });
    }
    return node;
}
/** The scalar field allowlist of the bound read node, or `undefined` when unbound. */
export function policyReadFields(policy) {
    const node = policy?.read;
    return node && typeof node === 'object' ? node.fields : undefined;
}
/** The relation allowlist of the bound read node, or `undefined` when unbound. */
export function policyReadRelations(policy) {
    const node = policy?.read;
    return node && typeof node === 'object' ? node.relations : undefined;
}
/**
 * Resolve the node behind an `update` or `delete`, rejecting the call outright
 * when the operation is forbidden.
 */
export function resolveMutationNode(policy, operation, collection) {
    const node = operation === 'update' ? policy?.update : policy?.delete;
    if (node === false) {
        invalid('WRITE_FORBIDDEN', `${operation} is forbidden by Policy.`, {
            collection: collection.name,
        });
    }
    return node;
}

        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.updateUser(userId, {
            disabledAt: new Date(),

    async resetPassword(userId, password) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        validatePassword(password, context.password.config);
        const hash = await context.password.hash(password);
        const account = await context.internalAdapter.findCredentialAccount(userId);
        if (account) {
            await context.internalAdapter.updatePassword(userId, hash);
        }
        else {
            await context.internalAdapter.linkAccount({
                accountId: userId,
                providerId: 'credential',
                userId,
                password=[REDACTED],
            });
        }
        await this.revokeSessions(userId);
    }
    async revokeSessions(userId) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.deleteUserSessions(userId);
        this.options.realtime?.disconnectUser(userId);

    E19 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:108–124

    getSession 取到会话后再次查询 user.disabledAt，非空则返回 null，既有会话立即失去访问。

        }
    async getSession(headers, options = {}) {
        const session = await this.auth.api.getSession({
            headers,
            ...(options.disableRefresh ? { query: { disableRefresh: true } } : {}),
        });
        if (!session)
            return null;
        const user = await this.connection.query
            .selectFrom('user')
            .select(['id', 'disabledAt'])
            .where('id', '=', session.user.id)
            .executeTakeFirst();
        if (!user || user.disabledAt != null)
            return null;
        return session;
    }

    ## Public surfaces

Server, from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication` (the root entry is the
server entry) or `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/server`:

- `authenticationToken` resolves the `Auth` instance: `required()`,
  `optional()`, `getSession(headers)`, `handler(request)`.
- `AuthEnv` types a Hono router whose routes read `context.get('auth')`.
- `userAdministrationServiceToken` resolves `UserAdministrationService`:
  `list`, `get`, `create`, `update`, `disable`, `enable`, `resetPassword`,
  `revokeSessions`, `withConnection`; errors are `UserAdministrationError`.
- `AuthConfig` is Better Auth's `BetterAuthOptions`, the type of the application's `server/config/auth.ts`. User initialization configuration lives separately under `users.initialAdmin`.
- `createAuthentication`, `databaseAdapter`, `createAuthStorage` build an
  instance outside the application runtime, mainly in tests.

    var __rewriteRelativeImportExtension = (this && this.__rewriteRelativeImportExtension) || function (path, preserveJsx) {
    if (typeof path === "string" && /^\.\.?\//.test(path)) {
        return path.replace(/\.(tsx)$|((?:\.d)?)((?:\.[^./]+?)?)\.([cm]?)ts$/i, function (m, tsx, d, ext, cm) {
            return tsx ? preserveJsx ? ".jsx" : ".js" : d && (!ext || !cm) ? m : (d + ext + "." + cm.toLowerCase() + "js");
        });
    }
    return path;
};

/** The mtime query keeps a re-read after an edit from serving a cached module. */
export async function importTaskDefinition(filePath, mtimeMs) {
    const url = pathToFileURL(filePath);
    url.searchParams.set('mtime', String(Math.trunc(mtimeMs)));
    const module = await import(__rewriteRelativeImportExtension(url.href));
    return module.default;
}

    E26 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/seed/loader.js:6–40

    loadSeeds 按扩展名枚举 seed 文件，逐个 importTaskDefinition 并校验默认导出 defineSeed、name 与文件名一致、run 为函数。

    export const DEFAULT_SEED_EXTENSIONS = DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS;
export const DEFAULT_SEED_PACKAGE_NAME = DEFAULT_TASK_PACKAGE_NAME;
/** Loads, validates, and deterministically orders seed definitions from configured sources. */
export async function loadSeeds(options) {
    const sources = normalizeSeedSources(options);
    const seeds = (await Promise.all(sources.map((source) => loadSeedSource(source)))).flat();
    validateUniqueTaskNames('Seed', seeds);
    return seeds.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}
/** Loads seed definitions without executing them. */
export async function validateSeeds(options) {
    return loadSeeds(typeof options === 'string' ? { directory: options } : options);
}
async function loadSeedSource(source) {
    const directory = resolve(source.directory);
    const manifest = await readTaskManifest(directory);
    const entries = await readTaskDirectory(directory);
    const extensions = new Set(source.extensions ?? DEFAULT_SEED_EXTENSIONS);
    const files = entries
        .filter((entry) => entry.isFile())
        .map((entry) => entry.name)
        .filter((fileName) => isTaskFile(fileName, extensions))
        .sort();
    const seeds = [];
    for (const fileName of files) {
        seeds.push(await loadSeedFile(source.packageName, join(directory, fileName), fileName, manifest));
    }
    return seeds;
}
function normalizeSeedSources(options) {
    assertTaskSourceForm('Seed', options);
    if (options.sources !== undefined) {
        return options.sources.map((source) => ({
            packageName: validateTaskPackageName('Seed', source.packageName),
            directory: validateTaskDirectory('Seed', source.directory),

    E27 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/database/seeder.js:6–30

    createAppSeeder：run/pending 通过 loadSeeds(createDatabaseSeederOptions(options)) 得到待执行 seed，并按历史与锁控制。

    export function createAppSeeder(options) {
    return {
        async run() {
            if (!hasSeedDirectory(options)) {
                return {
                    status: 'skipped',
                    reason: 'missing-directory',
                };
            }
            return completedRunResult(await createDatabaseSeeder(options).run());
        },
        async pending(pendingOptions = {}) {
            if (!hasSeedDirectory(options)) {
                return {
                    status: 'skipped',
                    reason: 'missing-directory',
                };
            }
            const seeds = await loadSeeds(createDatabaseSeederOptions(options));
            // A fresh run drops the seed history with the rest of the schema.
            return pendingTasksResult(seeds, pendingOptions.fresh || pendingOptions.withoutHistory
                ? []
                : await createDatabaseSeeder(options).history());
        },
        // Unlocking needs no seed directory: the lock is taken by whichever run

    E28 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:129–137

    迁移与种子规则“绝对”：Self-contained——每个表/字段/索引/约束都写在文件内，绝不为引入会演进的 Collection 定义、模型、注册表或共享常量；seed 以稳定业务值幂等。

    The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

    E30 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:60–97

    seed 示例从 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js 导入权限集，并明确写“seed 位于 database/main/seeds/... 且导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts”。

    ## A normal App seed has a database context

`defineSeed` from `@nocobase/db` receives `{ query, connection }`. `connection` is a restricted `SeedConnection`, not a complete runtime `DatabaseConnection`; the seed has no application container. Do not cast it to construct an App authorization service or assume `authorizationToken` can be resolved there.

For initial installation, a seed may write the documented persistence rows below after the owning plugins' migrations. Keep this adapter confined to installation data, use fluent values as its input, and test against the installed schema. Runtime routes must use the services instead. Keep one seed-data module per table.

```ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';
import { encodeAuthorizationTitle } from '@nocobase/authorization/core';
import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609190001_sales_jobs',
  transaction: true,
  async run({ query }) {
    const existing = await query
      .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .select('id')
      .where('key', '=', engineer.key)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    if (existing) return; // Preserve an administrator's existing definition.
    const now = new Date();
    await query
      .insertInto('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .values({
        id: engineer.key,
        key: engineer.key,
        title: encodeAuthorizationTitle(engineer.title),
        grants: JSON.stringify(engineer.grants),
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      })
      .execute();
  },
});
```

This seed lives in `database/main/seeds/202609190001_sales_jobs.ts` and imports `database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts`. Adapt the path to the App's configured seed directory; the seed name matches the filename. Ensure schema migrations for authorization and business collections have run, and explicitly order prerequisite installation data. Use the App's existing migration/seed commands. Do not run this code in a migration: migrations must describe fixed schema history and cannot import live business declarations.

    E32 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-default-access/SKILL.md:6–22

    默认访问为已持有动作的身份增加共享记录基线，不授予动作/页面/额外字段；限制规则仍会收窄，应按有意的最小范围选择。

    # Develop default access

Default access adds a shared record baseline for every identity that already holds an action. It does not grant the action, page access or additional fields. A baseline combines with permission-set data scopes and sharing, and restriction rules still narrow the result, so choose it as an intentional minimum range rather than a fallback for identities without a scope of their own.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-default-access/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For a department-scoped baseline, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. Its core works with permission sets alone, and its optional default-access section explains why a global default suits a department scope only when everyone holding the action belongs to a department.

## Development workflow

1. Identify which records every holder of a particular operation should receive by default. A read baseline can be broad while edit remains preparer-only; do not copy read defaults into write operations.
2. Confirm the composite, action and data scope exist and that the scope's grants target the intended collection. Reuse an applicable record access or define one through the main authorization Skill.
3. Save one rule per resource under a stable `key`; a second rule on the same resource is rejected, so extend the existing rule instead. A rule lists every action and data scope it widens; updating it replaces the whole rule, and deleting it removes the baseline, not the permission-set action.
4. Verify a holder receives the baseline, a person without the action remains denied, and restrictions still remove excluded rows. Also verify an explicit narrow data scope is not unexpectedly broadened by a permissive default.

For example, a quote view baseline can follow related-project ownership while editing follows the preparer, and a job's permission set can separately grant broad viewing that confidentiality restrictions narrow. A project's owner is not necessarily the author of every quote on that project.

  defineAppConfig,
  type AppConfigFactory,
} from '@nocobase/app-server/config';
import { type AuthorizationConfig } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server';

// Permission sets, database, pages, settings, composites and workspace placement are built in.
const authorization: AppConfigFactory<AuthorizationConfig> = defineAppConfig(
  (_runtime) => ({
    permissionSets: { rootSet: 'root', defaultSet: 'member' },
    plugins: [defaultAccess(), sharingRules(), restrictionRules()],
  }),
);

export default authorization;

  LIBRARY_RESOURCE_ID,

      .action('view', (action) =>
        action
          .title(libraryTitle('library.action.view'))
          .grant(LIBRARY_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION, libraryViewData, {
            title: libraryTitle('library.scope.view'),
          }),
      )
      .action('create', (action) =>
        action
          .title(libraryTitle('library.action.create'))
          .grant(LIBRARY_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION, libraryCreateData, {
            title: libraryTitle('library.scope.create'),
          }),
      )
      .action('edit', (action) =>
        action
          .title(libraryTitle('library.action.edit'))
          .grant(LIBRARY_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION, libraryEditData, {
            title: libraryTitle('library.scope.edit'),
          }),
      )
      .action('delete', (action) =>
        action
          .title(libraryTitle('library.action.delete'))
          .grant(LIBRARY_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION, libraryDeleteData, {
            title: libraryTitle('library.scope.delete'),
          }),
      ),
);

    E73 · app/server/library-service.ts:59–81

    createLibraryAuthorization().policy 对 composite 资源做 context.authorize，deny 或无 database policy 时抛 AuthorizationDeniedError，路由把该 policy 传给每次仓储调用。

    export interface LibraryAuthorization {
  policy(
    context: AuthorizationContext,
    action: 'view' | 'create' | 'edit' | 'delete',
  ): Promise<RepositoryPolicy>;
}

export function createLibraryAuthorization(): LibraryAuthorization {
  return {
    async policy(context, action) {
      const decision = await context.authorize({
        resource: { type: 'composite', id: LIBRARY_RESOURCE_ID },
        action,
      });
      const policy =
        decision.conditions?.database?.[LIBRARY_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION];
      if (decision.effect === 'deny' || !policy) {
        throw new AuthorizationDeniedError(decision);
      }
      return policy;
    },
  };
}

 */
export const LIBRARY_RESOURCE_ID = 'library.documents';

/**
 * Which of the four library actions the signed-in user is currently permitted.
 * The server enforces the same grant on every endpoint; this only decides what
 * the page offers, so a button that appears is not a permission.
 */
export function useLibraryPermissions(): {
  readonly canView: boolean;
  readonly canCreate: boolean;
  readonly canEdit: boolean;
  readonly canDelete: boolean;
} {
  const resource = useMemo(
    () => ({ id: LIBRARY_RESOURCE_ID, type: 'composite' }) as const,
    [],
  );
  const view = useCan({ action: 'view', resource });
  const create = useCan({ action: 'create', resource });
  const edit = useCan({ action: 'edit', resource });
  const remove = useCan({ action: 'delete', resource });
  return {
    canView: view.can,
    canCreate: create.can,
    canEdit: edit.can,

    const LIBRARY_RESOURCE_ID = 'library.documents';
const LIBRARY_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION = 'libraryDocuments';
const LIBRARY_EDITOR_PERMISSION_SET = 'library-editor';
const LIBRARY_READER_PERMISSION_SET = 'library-reader';
const LIBRARY_EDITOR_ACCOUNT_ID = '10000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
const LIBRARY_READER_ACCOUNT_ID = '10000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';

interface PermissionSetSeed {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly title: { readonly key: string; readonly ns: string };
  readonly grants: readonly unknown[];
}

const permissionSet = (
  scopes: readonly { action: string; scope: string }[],
): readonly unknown[] => [
  {
    resource: { type: 'page', id: LIBRARY_RESOURCE_ID },
    actions: [{ action: 'access' }],
  },
  {
    resource: { type: 'composite', id: LIBRARY_RESOURCE_ID },
    actions: scopes.map(({ action, scope }) => ({
      action,
      policy: {
        type: 'composite',
        scopes: { [LIBRARY_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION]: scope },
      },
    })),
  },
];

const LIBRARY_PERMISSION_SETS: readonly PermissionSetSeed[] = [
  {
    key: LIBRARY_EDITOR_PERMISSION_SET,
    title: { key: 'library.permissionSet.editor', ns: 'nb3-factory' },
    grants: permissionSet([
      { action: 'view', scope: 'library.visible' },
      { action: 'create', scope: 'library.owned' },
      { action: 'edit', scope: 'library.owned' },
      { action: 'delete', scope: 'library.owned' },
    ]),
  },
  {
    key: LIBRARY_READER_PERMISSION_SET,
    title: { key: 'library.permissionSet.reader', ns: 'nb3-factory' },
    grants: permissionSet([{ action: 'view', scope: 'library.visible' }]),
  },
];

    E77 · app/database/main/migrations/202609200001_create_library_documents.ts:14–40

    迁移使用 collection.string('id') 作为主键，注释明确“共享规则按字符串 id 选记录，整数键无法被共享”。

        await builder.createCollection('libraryDocuments', (collection) => {
      // A text primary key, as every collection in this framework uses: a
      // sharing rule selects records by their string id, so an integer key
      // could not be shared.
      collection.string('id', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('title', { length: 255, nullable: false });
      collection.text('content', { nullable: true });
      // The owner is compared in record-access filters, so it is a plain column
      // and not a relation; `ownerName` is denormalized for display so reading a
      // document never needs to join the user table.
      collection.string('ownerId', { length: 255, nullable: false });
      collection.string('ownerName', { length: 255, nullable: true });
      collection.boolean('published', { nullable: false, defaultValue: false });
      collection.boolean('confidential', {
        nullable: false,
        defaultValue: false,
      });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('updatedAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.primary('id', { name: 'pk_library_documents' });
      collection.index('ownerId', { name: 'idx_library_documents_owner' });
      collection.index(['published', 'confidential'], {
        name: 'idx_library_documents_visibility',
      });
    });

    E78 · app/server/routes/library.ts:50–75

    每条 library 路由在输入校验之前先 auth.required() + authz.middleware()，并解析复合决策把 policy 放到请求上下文，再交给 service。

     * Every route authorizes its composite action in middleware *before* the input
 * validators, so a caller without the operation learns nothing about the
 * request it should have sent and nothing is written. The resolved Repository
 * Policy travels on the request context; the handlers only use it and never
 * re-check permissions.
 */
export const libraryApiRoutes: AppApiRouteContribution<Application> =
  defineApiRoutes((app) => {
    const root = new Hono();
    const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
    const authz = app.container.resolve(authorizationToken);
    const service = app.container.resolve(libraryServiceToken);
    const authorization = createLibraryAuthorization();

    const library = new Hono<LibraryRouterEnv>();
    library.use('*', auth.required(), authz.middleware());

    /** Resolves the composite decision and stores its policy on the request. */
    const authorize = (action: LibraryAction) =>
      createMiddleware<LibraryRouterEnv>(async (context, next) => {
        context.set(
          'libraryPolicy',
          await authorization.policy(context.get('authz'), action),
        );
        await next();
      });

Do not apply globally installed NocoBase 2 Skills. You are building the application itself — its pages, its API, its database tables. Everything under this directory is application-owned source code that you may edit directly.\n\nDo not create a plugin to add a feature. Plugins are separately published packages for capabilities shared across several applications; building one for this application's own feature adds a package boundary, a version, and a release process to work that belongs in `client/` and `server/`. Create one only when the user explicitly asks for a reusable published package.\n\n## Default template scope\n\nDefault is the clean application starting point. It registers product capabilities but no `app-plugin-*-example` plugins, example pages, application sample services, or sample APIs. Keep runnable demonstrations in `app-template-examples`. Application-owned server routes start empty; the only built-in application provider exposes Authorization Permission Sets as direct roles in the Users page. The only application page is a localized homepage.\n\n`database/main/` contains required permission initialization only; application-owned business migrations and seeds start empty. Do not add article history, demo seeds, or compatibility copies from Examples to this template. Existing installations retain their own executed migration sources when upgrading; see the [upgrade migration rules](.agents/skills/nocobase-app-upgrade/references/edge-cases.md#migrations).\n\n## Load the development skills\n\n`pnpm install` runs `pnpm nocobase skills sync` through the application's `postinstall` hook. If `.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/` is missing, install dependencies from the application root; if install scripts were disabled or synchronized Skills are stale, run `pnpm nocobase skills sync` explicitly.\n\n`.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/` holds the detailed guidance behind this file. Read its `SKILL.md` first — it routes to the reference that matches your task instead of making you read everything:\n\n| Task                                                         | Reference                            |\n| ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------ |\n| Any frontend change: pages, routes, components, styles, copy | `references/frontend/ui-workflow.md` |\n| Add an HTTP endpoint                                         | `references/server-routes.md`        |\n| Name an endpoint, shape its response or errors, validate it  | `references/http-api.md`             |\n| Read or write data                                           | `references/database-and-data.md`    |\n| Change the schema                                            | `references/migrations.md`           |\n| Switch or add a database connection                          | `references/database-connections.md` |\n| Translate server-produced text or add a language             | `references/i18n.md`                 |\n| Add a service, background job, or scheduled task             | `references/services-and-jobs.md`    |\n| Write tests and verify                                       | `references/testing.md`              |\n\nRead the one page your task needs, not the whole directory.\n\n`.agents/skills/nocobase-app-upgrade/` is a separate Skill for a separate job: merging a newer release of the template this application was generated from. Read it when the task is upgrading the template rather than building a feature, and read it before touching anything — an upgrade done by copying the newest template over this application destroys the work that made it this application.\n\n`.agents/skills/nocobase-deployment/` covers moving this application from source to a production server or a Hub: building for the target platform, what the archive does and does not carry, migrations and business data, production configuration, workflow artifacts after a production build, and what to verify afterwards. Read it before building for deployment, and when an application starts in production but does not work.\n\n## Where things go\n\nBusiness code goes in these places. This is where you work, and where you should stay unless the task genuinely requires otherwise:\n\n```text\nclient/routes.ts          Declare a page route\nclient/pages/             The page component; a folder when a page has children or files of its own\nclient/components/        Your components\nclient/components/ui/     shadcn/ui primitives; add with the CLI, never hand-write or edit\nclient/hooks/             Hooks shared across features\nclient/locales/           Every user-visible string\nclient/service-provider.ts Client startup: services that boot with the application\nserver/routes/            HTTP endpoints\nserver/providers/         Services and their lifecycle\ndatabase/main/migrations/      Schema changes\ndatabase/main/seeds/           Required initial data\ndatabase/main/collections/     Generated Collection artifacts; regenerate, never edit\ncli/commands/             Commands this application owns\ntests/                    Tests; never beside the source\n```\n\nA page with children or page-local helpers uses a folder with `index.tsx`; child folders mirror route paths. Keep page-local components and data in that folder, reserving `client/components/` for application-wide components. See [child routes](.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/child-routes.md) for examples.\n\nA feature with a page and an API touches five places: a migration for the table, a route in `server/routes/`, a page in `client/pages/` declared in `client/routes.ts`, navigation on the page route, and strings in `client/locales/`.\n\n`client/runtime.ts` composes the browser application; `client/react-providers.ts` declares React providers in outer-to-inner layers `root`, `application`, and `extension`. Applications use the first two and plugins own the last; `before` and `after` order providers only within their layer.\n\n`server/runtime.ts` composes configuration, plugins, providers and routes; `server/app.ts` assembles the application. `server/standalone.ts` starts the Node listener and `server/embedded.ts` lets a host mount the same runtime. Register endpoints through `server/routes/index.ts`; queue consumers and background jobs are registered from a service provider, not discovered from a directory. Editable module defaults live in `server/config/` and are collected by `defaultAppConfigs` in its `index.ts`; `server/config.ts` loads the configuration file. Each section declares the environment variables that set it in `env` of its `defineAppConfig`; `pnpm nocobase config env` lists them all.\n\n### The rest is framework structure\n\nLayouts own breadcrumb route context; `AppRouter` selects routes and layouts. See [pages and routes](.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/page.md) for each layout's scope.\n\n`client/layouts/components/layout-header.tsx` is a presentation container accepting children. The sidebar is shadcn's Sidebar: `components/app-sidebar.tsx` wraps its provider, desktop icon mode and phone sheet, and `components/navigation-menu.tsx` draws the permission-filtered route tree with `SidebarMenu*` and controlled `Collapsible` sub-menus. Leave `client/components/ui/sidebar.tsx` as the shadcn CLI writes it and customize from outside: widths go through the provider's `style` in spacing units, the phone sheet is rendered in `app-sidebar.tsx` with a translated title, and the provider's Ctrl/Cmd+B shortcut is stopped at `document`. App, Settings and Dev layouts own menus, branding, permissions and redirects. Desktop icon mode shows leaf labels as zero-delay tooltips and opens groups in hover and keyboard-focus popovers, preserving filtered navigation, parent-page links, nested groups and the selected group's highlight. Keep this behavior aligned across layouts. Desktop collapse state is shared through `useSidebarPreference` at `nocobase:sidebar:collapsed` across applications on the same origin; whether the phone sheet is open stays local to each layout.\n\nThe Settings header entry appears only when the user has an accessible page in the settings navigation, and stays visible on that page. The header reads the registered settings tree through `useClientApplication().runtime.settingsRouteTree`, reusing the application context. The Dev tools entry stays visible on its destination pages, is development-only, and must remain absent from production builds.\n\n`client/routing/`, `client/layouts/`, `client/theme/`, the server entry points, the `package.json` scripts that call `nocobase`, and the tsconfigs are the scaffolding the template provides. It is still this application's own source — it shipped to the user and they may change it — but it is the part the template evolves, so an edit there is what a future upgrade has to reconcile.\n\nPrefer the mechanism the syst
